// One bss record of 0x20 bytes, not 26 scalars (.agents/research/hard-cases.md section 4):
// SsUtKeyOn and SsUtKeyOnV fill it byte by byte, SpuVmDoAllocate computes &EA28 - 2. All three
// turned out to be LIBSND VMANAGER (2026-10-15), so this is a LIBSND variable, not game data.
// Its neighbours: 0x8008EA0A is LIBGPU's (psyq/80020C74), 0x8008EA2C LIBSND's.
D_8008EA0C = 0x8008EA0C; // size:0x20

// Records that splat cut into fake labels from lwl/lwr and field offsets (work/config-window.md).
g_tileOffsets = 0x80087F30; // size:0xE0 // 6-byte colour+s16 records, D_80087F33/34 were the lwl/lh halves // 6-byte x y z offsets in a tile copied into StagePlace +4 by Link_Find and the spawn pickers
g_spawnPlaceOffsets = 0x80088D3C; // size:0x1DC // same 6-byte records, D_80088D3F/40 were the lwl/lh halves // 6-byte records copied by Sym_SpawnAtPlace into the placement query, four bytes then the height
g_sceneryParams = 0x8008E0A4; // size:0x20 // one record filled by func_80055258/func_80055410, passed whole to func_80056320 // filled by func_80055258 and its siblings then passed to func_80056320 and copied by the object's init
// Jump table without the zero word after it: that word is the .align 3 pad of jtbl_80011594.
jtbl_8001157C = 0x8001157C; // size:0x14
// 8-byte sdata pairs copied whole as structs, defined with their initializer in their C unit (splat.yaml .sdata).
g_viewDefaultScreenSize = 0x8008A8FC; // size:0x8 // ScreenPoint (0x100, 0xF0), copied to View +0x34 at reset
g_bandPos = 0x8008AB68; // size:0x8 // band position, y + 30 for layout 2
g_bandSize = 0x8008AB70; // size:0x8 // band size 320 x 144

g_renderObjLightMode = 0x8008E24C; // Render_SortModel stores bits 3 and 4 of the object's GsDOBJ2 attribute there (the per-object light mode), and a non-zero value selects the depth cue shift 9 (RAM run, 165529 sorted models, light mode 1 with object bits 3-4 = 1 in the dream)
g_renderUseLightMode = 0x8008E250; // Render_SortModel stores bit 5 of the object's GsDOBJ2 attribute there, and while it is set a non-zero g_gsLightMode selects the depth cue shift 9
GsIDMATRIX = 0x8008E98C; // libgs valiable_init writes 0x1000 at +0x0, +0x8 and +0x10 and zero at +0x14 to +0x1C (the identity MATRIX) and GsInitCoordinate2 copies it into new coordinates, the PSY-Q libgs global of that role is GsIDMATRIX, StageMap_ComputeFlatView starts from it
g_type74ClearColor = 0x8008AC1C; // the word (0, 100, 190) that Behavior_Type74 hands at its first tick to the DreamSys camera's slot 0x64 View_SetClearColor, the background colour behind the scene, type 74 spawns only in stage 10 (slot key 2, place 19), in RAM (run a74, stage 10 forced) Behavior_Type74 called View_SetClearColor with (0, 100, 190) at its tick 0, the stage had just set white, and the sky pixels of the capture read (0, 98, 189)
g_fallTurn = 0x8008ABF0; // the word (180, 1), one angle as a degree fraction like the rotation records, returned by Link_GetFallTurn and kept at DreamSys +0x880 by DreamSys_LinkFall
Link_GetFallTurn = 0x8005BF48; // type:func size:0x20 // returns &D_8008ABF0 (180 over 1) unless g_linkToStage is 12, its only caller DreamSys_LinkFall stores it at DreamSys +0x880
g_menuTitlePos = 0x8008A9B4; // the position (-4, -23) Menu_ShowItems gives to the title label's show slot 0x4C, the same two-word form as g_pauseTextPos
Movie_SetAutoStart = 0x80045E3C; // type:func size:0x8 // g_movieVtbl slot 0x6C, only writes Movie +0x68 which Movie_Open reads to request the first stream start, RAM 1 for both boot videos
g_cardMessageRect = 0x80086EC4; // three words 0, 160, 120 passed as the sprite rect of ScreenSprite_Create for the card message picture in Card_ShowMessage, same layout as g_inputBoxRect and g_stringListBgRect
g_seqTicksPerSecond = 0x8009024C; // LIBSND, SsSetTickMode stores 60 or 50 by video mode, 120, 240 or the rate itself above 5, _SsInit stores 60, the tempo code (Snd_tempo, _SsInitSoundSeq, GetMetaEvent) divides by it times 60, RAM 60 on the Japanese console
g_seqOpenMask = 0x8008EA00; // one bit per open sequence, SsSeqOpen takes the first clear bit and prints "Can't Open Sequence data any more" when it is -1, seq_close clears the bit, SsSeqCalledTbyT ticks only sequences whose bit is set, _SsInit clears it, RAM 0 at the menu
g_soundBusy = 0x8008E934; // SsUtKeyOn, SsUtKeyOff, SsUtKeyOnV, SsUtKeyOffV and SsSeqCalledTbyT return at once when it is 1, set it while they run and clear it before returning, _SsInit clears it
g_seqNrpnCallbacks = 0x80090368; // 32 rows of 16 function pointers indexed by ContNrpn1's two arguments and called by it when an NRPN data entry has type 0x28, _SsInit clears them all, nothing in the executable writes one
Object_EmptySlot34 = 0x80018350; // type:func size:0x8 // slot 0x34 of g_objectVtbl and of 57 other vtables in the data, empty body, nothing calls the slot
File_EmptySlot60 = 0x80026C80; // type:func size:0x8 // slot 0x60 of g_fileBaseVtbl and 16 other file class vtables in the data, empty body
Pad_EmptySlot4C = 0x80025E14; // type:func size:0x8 // g_padVtbl slot 0x4C in the data, empty body
Pad_EmptySlot54 = 0x80025E94; // type:func size:0x8 // g_padVtbl slot 0x54 in the data, empty body
